\displaystyle+\sum_{i=j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\Big]}_{=~(d+2)~\bm{I},~\mathrm{by}~\mathrm{lemma}~\ref {statistical_properties}}

\displaystyle=\Big(\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\Big)^{2}-\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\Big)\Big)\bm{I}

\displaystyle+\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\Big)(d+2)\bm{I}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\cdot\bm{I}

Here,
\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}=\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\Big)^{2}-\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\Big)
for the third equality.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-j}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-j}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

Notice that
\bm{w}
appears three (odd) times in the second equality,
if we define a function
g(\bm{w})=\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}
,
we can see that
g(-\bm{w})=-g(\bm{w})
, and further
\mathbb{E}_{\bm{w}}\Big[g(\bm{w})\Big]=\bm{0}
.
Therefore, the above expectation equals to
\bm{0}
.
We will use the similar property in some of the following equations.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]+\sum_{i=j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{4}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\Big)^{2}-\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\Big)\Big)d^{2}+\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\Big)\cdot 3d(d+2)

\displaystyle=\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}

where the second equality is by
\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]=\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]=d
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{4}\Big]=3d(d+2)
(lemma
A.2
).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]}_{=~\bm{I}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]}_{=~\bm{I}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\cdot\bm{I}

\displaystyle=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\cdot\bm{I}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\bm{w}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

Notice that
\bm{w}
appears three (odd) times in the second equality,
thus this expectation equals to
\bm{0}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle+\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}+\bm{0}=\bm{0}

Notice that
\bm{x}_{N-i}
appears three (odd) times in
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]
,
and
\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}
appears once (odd) in
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]
,
thus this expectation equals to
\bm{0}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-j}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

Notice that
\bm{w}
appears three (odd) times in the second equality,
thus this expectation equals to
\bm{0}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\Big]+\sum_{i=j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-j}}\Big]+\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{2i+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{d\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}

Notice that
\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}
appears once (odd) in
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}\Big]
where
i\neq j
,
thus
\sum_{i\neq j}\alpha^{i+j+2}\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\Big]=0
.
Moreover,
\mathbb{E}\Big[y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]=d
by lemma
A.2
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{N-i}}_{y_{N-i}}\Big]

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

C.3
Proof of Lemma
A.4

Lemma C.3
(restatement of lemma
A.4
)

The gradient of trainable parameters
\bm{\theta}^{\prime}=\{\bm{B},\bm{C},\bm{b},\bm{b}_{B},\bm{b}_{C}\}
with respect to loss (Eq. (
23
)) are as follows:

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}_{B}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\bm{0},

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}_{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\bm{0},

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{B}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\Big(\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{1}}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}-\underbrace{\alpha\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)}_{:=\beta_{3}}\bm{C},

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\underbrace{\Big(d^{2}\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{2}}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b},

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\Big(\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{1}}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Big(d^{2}\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{2}}\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\alpha\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)}_{:=\beta_{3}}\bm{B}.

Proof of lemma
C.3
.
Recalling the loss:

\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)^{2}\Big]

We will compute the gradient of
\{\bm{B},\bm{C},\bm{b},\bm{b}_{B},\bm{b}_{C}\}
with respect to
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})
.
Some expectation calculation are detailed in Section
C.3.1
.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}_{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\underbrace{\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})}_{:=\bm{v}}-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\underbrace{\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})}_{:=\bm{v}}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{v}\bm{v}^{\top}(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})\Big]-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{v}\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{v}\bm{v}^{\top}\bm{C}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\Big]+(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{v}\bm{v}^{\top}\Big]\bm{b}_{C}-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{v}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\Big]

It is clear that
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\Big]=\bm{0}
.
Thus, if
\bm{b}_{C}=\bm{0}
,
then
\nabla_{\bm{b}_{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\bm{0}
.
Notice that we assume
\bm{b}_{C}(0)=\bm{0}
at initialization,
so by induction,
\bm{b}_{C}(t)=\bm{0}
and
\nabla_{\bm{b}_{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}(t))=\bm{0}
for
t\geq 0
.
We will consider
\bm{b}_{C}=\bm{0}
when computing other gradients.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}_{B}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{d\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1+\alpha)}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}_{B}

The last equality follows from lemma
C.4
where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]=\frac{d\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}_{B}
,
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]=\bm{0}
.
Similar to
\bm{b}_{C}
, notice that
\bm{b}_{B}
is initialized as
\bm{0}
, thus by induction,
\bm{b}_{B}(t)=\bm{0}
and
\nabla_{\bm{b}_{B}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta}(t))=\bm{0}
for
t\geq 0
.
We will consider
\bm{b}_{B}=\bm{b}_{C}=\bm{0}
when computing other gradients.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{C}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\Big(\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{1}}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\Big(d^{2}\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{2}}\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\alpha\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)}_{:=\beta_{3}}\bm{B}

The last equality follows from lemma
C.4
, where we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\cdot\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{B}

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{B}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\Big(\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{1}}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\alpha\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)}_{:=\beta_{3}}\bm{C}

The last equality follows from lemma
C.4
, where we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}

and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{C}
.

\displaystyle\nabla_{\bm{b}}\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}+\bm{b}_{C})\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[(1-\alpha)\Big((1-\alpha)(\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q})^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})-\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=(1-\alpha)^{2}\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle-(1-\alpha)\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\Big(d^{2}\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}(1-\alpha)\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1+\alpha)}\Big)}_{:=\beta_{2}}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}

The last equality follows from lemma
C.4
, where we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}

and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]=\bm{0}
.

C.3.1
Auxiliary Lemma for Lemma
A.4

Lemma C.4

If vectors
\bm{x}_{i}
,
\bm{x}_{q}
and
\bm{w}
are iid generated from
\mathcal{N}(0,\bm{I}_{d})
,
y=\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{w}
we have the following expectations:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\cdot\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{B}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{C}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]=\bm{0}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\Big)\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{d\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}_{B}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]=\bm{0}

Proof of lemma
C.4
. We will use the results of lemma
A.3
to prove the above equation.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\big(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}\big)

\displaystyle\underbrace{\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)^{\top}}_{\spadesuit}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}\Big(\big(\bm{B}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{b}\big)

\displaystyle\big(y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{B}^{\top}+y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{b}^{\top}\big)\Big)\Big]\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\Big]\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\bm{b}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-j}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-j}\Big]\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{C}

\displaystyle+\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{b}\bm{b}^{\top}\bm{C}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\Big]=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{I}
,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]=\bm{0}
and

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]=\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}}_{\spadesuit}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle+\bm{b}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{B}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{I}
,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{w}\Big]=\bm{0}
, and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\bm{I}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\underbrace{\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\Big]=\Big(\frac{\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{N}\big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(d+1)\alpha^{2}\big(1-\alpha^{2N}\big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{I}
,
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\bm{I}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{\spadesuit}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{w}\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{C}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{x}_{N-i}\bm{w}^{\top}\Big]=\alpha\Big(\frac{1-\alpha^{N}}{1-\alpha}\Big)\bm{I}
,
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\bm{I}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\underbrace{\Big(\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}^{2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]=\bm{0}
,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}^{2}\Big]=\Big(\frac{d^{2}\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{N}\Big)^{2}}{(1-\alpha)^{2}}+\frac{(2d^{2}+6d)\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\Big)
,
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\bm{I}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}^{2}\bm{w}\Big]=\bm{0}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{C}^{\top}\Big(\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}(\bm{B}\bm{x}_{N-i}+y_{N-i}\bm{b}+\bm{b}_{B})\Big)}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{j+1}y_{N-j}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\bm{x}_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle+\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}\Big]

\displaystyle+\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}_{B}\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{d\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}\bm{C}\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}_{B}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have:
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}\bm{x}_{N-i}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-i}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-i}}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{N-j}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{y_{N-j}}\Big]=\bm{0}
,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}^{2}y_{N-j}\Big]=0
,

\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\sum_{j=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+j+2}y_{N-i}y_{N-j}\Big]=\frac{d\alpha^{2}\Big(1-\alpha^{2N}\Big)}{(1-\alpha)(1+\alpha)}
,
and
\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]=\bm{I}
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[\underbrace{\bm{w}^{\top}\bm{x}_{q}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{C}\bm{x}_{q}\underbrace{\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\bm{w}}_{\spadesuit}\cdot\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{C}\mathbb{E}\Big[\bm{x}_{q}\bm{x}_{q}^{\top}\Big]\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{w}\Big]

\displaystyle=\bm{0}

The last equality follows from lemma
A.3
, where we have
\mathbb{E}\Big[\sum_{i=0}^{N-1}\alpha^{i+1}y_{N-i}\bm{w}\Big]=\bm{0}
.

C.4
Proof of claim
B.1

This Section presents the bounds for terms
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(Tt+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)
and
\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)
,
establishing the property
\mathcal{A}(T+1)
.

Recurring the
Vector-coupled Dynamics
equations of
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}_{i}(t+1)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{c}_{i}(t+1)
and
\bm{b}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}(t+1)
in lemma
A.7
,
we have:

\displaystyle\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)=\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)+2\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)

\displaystyle-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)^{2}\Big)+\eta^{2}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(T)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big(2\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)+2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ III}}

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)=\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)+2\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)

\displaystyle-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\big)^{2}-\beta_{2}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\big)^{2}\Big)+\eta^{2}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(T)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big(2\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\big)^{2}

\displaystyle-\beta_{2}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}\Big)+\eta^{2}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{c}_{i}(0)+2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle-2\eta\beta_{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ IV}}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ V}}

\displaystyle\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)=\bm{b}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-2\eta\Big(\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\big)^{2}\Big)+\eta^{2}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\bm{b}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)+2\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=\textrm{term ~ IV}}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ VI}}

To bound terms I - VI,
we will use some inequalities from property
\mathcal{B}(t)
and lemma
C.7
as following with
i,j,k\in[1,d],i\neq j
:

|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)|\leq\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\frac{\delta(t)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{k}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{k}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),

\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|_{2}^{2}\leq 16d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),

Next we begin bounding terms I - VI.

Bound of term I:

By
\Big|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\Big|\leq\delta(s)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
, we have:

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta(s)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)+\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\delta(s)+2\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5\delta(s)}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 6\delta(s)

The third inequality is by
\delta(s)\geq 2\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}\geq 2\alpha=2\alpha=2\exp((-\ln 2)/N)
.
For the last inequality, as long as
N\geq\frac{2\ln 2}{\ln 6-\ln 5}
, we have
\frac{5}{\alpha^{2}}\leq 6
.

\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T}6\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)

\displaystyle\leq 6\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
is monotone decreasing.

Bound of term II:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(2\delta(s)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
is monotone decreasing.

Bound of term III:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T}8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
is monotone decreasing.

Bound of term IV:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big(2\delta(s)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{\delta(s)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\max}^{2}\cdot\Big(4\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{4}{\beta_{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma s)+\frac{1}{\beta_{2}^{2}}\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{\max}^{2}\cdot\Big(4\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)ds+\frac{4}{\beta_{2}}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\beta_{2}^{2}}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{2\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}+\frac{4\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{2}(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma}+\frac{\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{2\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}^{2}\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{17\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)
,
\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma s)
and
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
are monotone decreasing.
The last inequality is by
\frac{\exp(\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma)}{(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})}\leq 2

and
\frac{\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{2\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}\leq 7
since
\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq\exp(\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma)\leq 2
and
\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}\geq 1
,
\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\geq\frac{1}{7}
.

Bound of term V:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big(8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle=\frac{8d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{40\beta_{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{80\beta_{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)
and
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
are monotone decreasing.

Bound of term VI:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big(16d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 16d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)ds+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\int_{-1}^{\infty}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16d_{h}\beta_{2}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\gamma}+\frac{2d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

The second inequality is due to
\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)
and
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
are monotone decreasing.

We next use the bounds of I - VI to bound
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)
and
\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)
.

Lower bound of
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)}_{\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}}+2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\geq 0}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-2\eta\cdot\frac{6\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}-2\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot\frac{2\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-\frac{12\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}-\frac{4(d-1)\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-\frac{2*12*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}-\frac{2*4(d-1)*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{d_{h}}{2}

The third inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
,
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq 2
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq\big(1728\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+576(d-1)\beta_{1}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\big)/\beta_{1}
.

Upper bound of
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)}_{\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}}-2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ III}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+2\eta\cdot\frac{6\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}+\eta^{2}\cdot\frac{8d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+\frac{12\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{8\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+\frac{2*12*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}+\frac{2*8\eta d_{h}d^{2}*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\leq 2d_{h}

The third inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
,
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq 2
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from

\displaystyle d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})

\displaystyle\geq\big(576\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+192\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\big)/\beta_{1}

\displaystyle\geq\big(576\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+384\eta d_{h}d^{2}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\big)/\beta_{1}

Lower bound of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{c}_{i}(0)}_{\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}}+2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=~\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle-2\eta\beta_{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ IV}}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\geq 0}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-2\eta\cdot\frac{6\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}-2\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot\frac{2\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}-2\eta\beta_{2}\cdot\frac{17\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-\frac{2*12*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}-\frac{2*4(d-1)*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle-\frac{34*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{d_{h}}{2}

The second inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
,
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq 2
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq\big(1728\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+(576d+1872)\beta_{1}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\big)/\beta_{1}
.

Upper bound of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{i}(T+1)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{c}_{i}(0)}_{\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}}+2\eta\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)}_{\textrm{term ~ I}}-2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=\textrm{term ~ II}}

\displaystyle-2\eta\beta_{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{\geq 0}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ V}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+2\eta\cdot\frac{6\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}+2\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot\frac{2\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\cdot\big(\frac{16d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{80\beta_{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\gamma}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+\frac{2*12*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}+\frac{2*4(d-1)*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle+\frac{2*16\eta d_{h}d^{2}*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}+\frac{2*80\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\leq 2d_{h}

The second inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
,
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq 2
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from

\displaystyle d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})

\displaystyle\geq 576\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}+192(d-1)\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)

\displaystyle+384\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}+3840\ln 2\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)

\displaystyle\geq 576\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}+192(d-1)\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)

\displaystyle+768\eta d_{h}d^{2}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}+3840\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)

Lower bound of
\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)=\underbrace{\bm{b}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)}_{\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}}-2\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=\textrm{term ~ IV}}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\geq 0}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-2\eta\beta_{2}d\cdot\frac{17\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-\frac{34d\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3d_{h}}{4}-\frac{34d*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{d_{h}}{2}

The third inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq 2448d\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)
.

Upper bound of
\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)

\displaystyle\bm{b}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)=\underbrace{\bm{b}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)}_{\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}}-2\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\big(\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\big)^{2}}_{=\textrm{term ~ IV}}+\eta^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{s=0}^{T}\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(s)\Big\|_{2}^{2}}_{\textrm{term ~ VI}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+2\eta\beta_{2}d\cdot\frac{17\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}+\eta^{2}\cdot\big(\frac{16d_{h}\beta_{2}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\gamma}+\frac{2d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+\frac{34d\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\gamma}+\frac{2*16\eta d_{h}\beta_{2}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\gamma}+\frac{2*2\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}^{2}}{\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5d_{h}}{4}+\frac{34d*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}+\frac{32\eta d_{h}\beta_{2}d^{2}*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\eta d_{h}d^{2}*9d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}{\beta_{1}\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}

\displaystyle\leq 2d_{h}

The second inequality is by
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)\leq\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)\leq 2

The third inequality is by
\delta_{\max}=3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality follows from

\displaystyle d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})

\displaystyle\geq 816d\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+768\ln 2d^{2}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+48\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}

\displaystyle\geq 816d\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+768\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)+96\eta d_{h}d^{2}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)/\beta_{1}

C.5
Proof of claim
B.2

This Section presents the exponential decay bounds for terms
\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)
and
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)
,
establishing the property
\mathcal{B}(T+1)
.

Bound of
\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)

Recall the following equation from lemma
A.7
.

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}_{i}(t+1)

\displaystyle=\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)

\displaystyle-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)+\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{c}_{i}(t)

\displaystyle-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)

Based on the above equation, we have:

\begin{split}&\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|=\Big|\underline{\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)}\\
&\quad-\eta\beta_{1}\Big(\big(\underline{\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)}\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\\
&\quad-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\big(\underline{\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)}\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)\\
&\quad-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{k}(T)\Big)-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(T)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)\big)\Big)\big(\underline{\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)}\big)\\
&\quad+\eta\beta_{1}^{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)+\eta\beta_{1}^{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{k}(T)\\
&\quad+\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(T)\Big|\end{split}

(31)

The term
\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)
is highlighted with underline,
and we collect its
negative feedback
terms together.
The factor
\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)\big)\Big)\leq 1
will drive
\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)
to converge to zero.

By Recurring (Eq. (
31
)) from
0
to
T
, we have:

\begin{split}&\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\prod_{s=0}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)\big)\Big)\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)\big)\\
&\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\prod_{s^{\prime}=s+1}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{b}_{i}(s^{\prime})+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{c}_{i}(s^{\prime})\big)\Big)\\
&\quad\cdot\Big(\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}^{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)+\eta\beta_{1}^{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{k}(s)}_{\spadesuit}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\cdot\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)}_{\clubsuit}-\underbrace{\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)}_{\diamondsuit}\Big)\Big|\end{split}

(32)

Here
\prod_{s=0}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)\big)\Big)\leq(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T+1}
since
\gamma\leq\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s),\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)
.
Besides, from property
\mathcal{B}(0),\dots,\mathcal{B}(T)
and lemma
C.7
we know that
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)
and
\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)
have bounds with exponential decreasing rate.
Therefore, it is easy to derive an exponential decreasing upper bound for
\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|
.

By substituting the bounds of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)
,
\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(s)
,
\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{k}(s)
and
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)
, we have:

\begin{split}&\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|\\
&\leq(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T+1}\Big|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)\Big|\\
&\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T-s}\cdot\Big(\underbrace{2\eta\beta_{1}^{2}(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\spadesuit}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\cdot\big(2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{\delta(s)^{2}}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\big)}_{\clubsuit}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\big(8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\big)}_{\diamondsuit}\Big)\end{split}

(33)

The notations
\spadesuit
,
\clubsuit
and
\diamondsuit
highlight the corresponding terms between (Eq. (
32
)) and (Eq. (
33
)) for refference.

We further have the following:

\begin{split}&\quad\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|\\
&\leq(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T+1}\Big|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)\Big|\\
&\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T-s}\cdot\Big(\underbrace{2\eta\beta_{1}^{2}(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\spadesuit}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\cdot\big(2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{\delta(s)^{2}}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\big)}_{\clubsuit}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\big(8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\big)}_{\diamondsuit}\Big)\\
&\leq\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\Big|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{i}(0)\Big|\\
&\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\Big(\big(4\eta\beta_{1}^{2}(d-1)\delta(s)^{2}+\eta\beta_{1}\delta(s)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{1}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\\
&\quad+\big(2\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\delta(s)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(s)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\Big)\\
&\leq(\beta_{3}+\beta_{1}\delta(0))\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\\
&\quad+\Big(4\eta\beta_{1}^{2}(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+\eta\beta_{1}\delta(T)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{1}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\\
&\quad+\Big(2\eta\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\delta(T)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{3}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\\
&\leq\Big(\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}+\beta_{1}+\frac{8\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{2\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{6\beta_{1}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big)\\
&\quad\cdot\delta(T)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\\
&\leq\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\end{split}

(34)

The second inequality is derived by factoring out the factors
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
and
\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)
.
The third inequality is due to
\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

and
\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\leq\frac{3}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))
in lemma
C.5
.
The fourth inequality is by
\delta(0)\leq\delta(T)
,
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))
,
and we consider
\beta_{3}=\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}\cdot\delta(0)\leq\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}\cdot\delta(T)
.
The fifth inequality is by proving
\Big(\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}+\beta_{1}+\frac{8\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{2\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{6\beta_{1}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big)\leq 1

as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}+\beta_{1}+\frac{8\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{2\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{6\beta_{1}\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\delta(T)}{\gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta_{3}}{\delta(0)}+\frac{3}{4}+\frac{\delta(T)}{\gamma}\cdot\Big(8\beta_{1}(d-1)+2+16\eta d_{h}d^{2}+6\beta_{1}+24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta_{3}}{2\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}}+\frac{3}{4}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}\cdot\Big(8\beta_{1}(d-1)+2+16\eta d_{h}d^{2}+6\beta_{1}+24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 1

The first inequality is by
\beta_{1}\leq\frac{3}{4}
.
The second inequality is by
\delta(0)\geq 2\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
,
\delta(T)\leq 3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}
and
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality hold as long as
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}}+24\sqrt{\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}\big(8\beta_{1}(d-1)+2+8+6\beta_{1}+12\eta\beta_{1}/d\big)\Big)^{2}\geq\Big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}}+24\sqrt{\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}\big(8\beta_{1}(d-1)+2+16\eta d_{h}d^{2}+6\beta_{1}+24\eta\beta_{1}d_{h}d\big)\Big)^{2}
.
Therefore, we have

\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)\Big|\leq\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\leq\delta(T+1)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

(35)

where the last inequality is by
\delta(T)\leq\delta(T+1)
.

The proof for the bounds of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)
and
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)
are similar to that of
\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{i}(T+1)\big)
.
We presents the calculation as follows.

Bound of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)

\displaystyle\quad\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{j}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{k}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(T)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)+\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\Big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)-\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{j}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{k}(T)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(T)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\prod_{s=0}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)+\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{j}(s)\big)\Big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{j}(0)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\prod_{s^{\prime}=s+1}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{1}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{c}_{i}(s^{\prime})+\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{b}_{j}(s^{\prime})\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Big(\underbrace{\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{j}(s)+\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{j}(s)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{j}(s)}_{\spadesuit}

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{j}(s)-\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{j}(s)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{k}(s)}_{\clubsuit}

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\eta\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\cdot\bm{b}_{j}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)}_{\diamondsuit}+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(s)}_{\heartsuit}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T+1}\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{j}(0)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}(1-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)^{T-s}\big(\underbrace{2\eta\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\spadesuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\cdot 2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\clubsuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{2}\cdot\big(2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{\delta(s)^{2}}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\big)}_{\diamondsuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\big(8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\big)}_{\heartsuit}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}_{j}(0)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\Big(\big(2\eta\delta(s)^{2}+4\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta(s)^{2}+\eta\delta(s)^{2}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(s)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)

\displaystyle\quad+\big(2\eta\beta_{2}\delta(s)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(s)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(2\eta\delta(T)^{2}+4\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta(T)^{2}+\eta\delta(T)^{2}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(2\eta\beta_{2}\delta(T)^{2}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{3}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle=\Big(1+\frac{4\delta(T)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{8(d-2)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{2\delta(T)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)}{\beta_{1}\gamma}+\frac{6\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{24\eta d_{h}d\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta(T+1)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

(36)

This bound requires
\frac{\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big(\frac{4}{\beta_{1}}+8(d-2)+\frac{2}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}}{\beta_{1}}+6+24\eta d_{h}d\Big)\leq 1
,
which can be verified by
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(\frac{4}{\beta_{1}}+8(d-2)+\frac{2}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{2}{\beta_{1}}+6+\frac{12}{d}\Big)^{2}\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(\frac{4}{\beta_{1}}+8(d-2)+\frac{2}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}}{\beta_{1}}+6+24\eta d_{h}d\Big)^{2}
.

Bound of
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)

\displaystyle\quad\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{b}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\Big(1-\eta\beta_{2}\big(\bm{b}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)\big)\Big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)

\displaystyle\quad-\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{i}(T)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big|

\displaystyle=\Big|\prod_{s=0}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{2}\big(\bm{b}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)\big)\Big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\prod_{s^{\prime}=s+1}^{T}\Big(1-\eta\beta_{2}\big(\bm{b}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{b}(s^{\prime})+\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s^{\prime})\bm{c}_{i}(s^{\prime})\big)\Big)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\Big(\underbrace{\eta\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{i}(s)\big)\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)}_{\spadesuit}-\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}_{k}(s)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)}_{\clubsuit}

\displaystyle\quad-\underbrace{\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{b}(s)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(s)\bm{c}_{i}(s)}_{\diamondsuit}+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(s)\bm{\bar{b}}(s)}_{\heartsuit}\Big)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq(1-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)^{T+1}\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}(1-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)^{T-s}\big(\underbrace{\eta\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\spadesuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)}_{\clubsuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta\beta_{2}(d-1)\cdot\big(2\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)+\frac{\delta(s)^{2}}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\big)}_{\diamondsuit}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\eta^{2}\big(4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)+28\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(s)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\big)}_{\heartsuit}\big)

\displaystyle\leq\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)\Big|

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(s-T))\Big(\big(\eta\delta(T)^{2}+2\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+4\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)

\displaystyle\quad+\big(2\eta\beta_{2}(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+28\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(s)^{2}\big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(\eta\delta(T)^{2}+2\eta\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+\eta(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+4\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\quad+\Big(2\eta\beta_{2}(d-1)\delta(T)^{2}+28\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(T)^{2}\Big)\cdot\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle=\delta(T)\Big(1+\frac{4(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{56\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\Big(\frac{2\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{4\beta_{1}(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{2(d-1)\delta(T)}{\gamma}+\frac{8\eta d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq 2\delta(T+1)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))+\frac{\delta(T+1)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

(37)

This bound requires
\frac{\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big(4(d-1)+56\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}d\Big)\leq 1
and
\frac{\delta(T)}{\gamma}\Big(2+4\beta_{1}(d-1)+2(d-1)+8\eta d_{h}d^{2}\Big)\leq 1
,
which can be verified by
d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(4(d-1)+56d\ln 2\Big)^{2}\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(4(d-1)+56\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}d\Big)^{2}

and

d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(2+4\beta_{1}(d-1)+2(d-1)+4\Big)^{2}\geq 36\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(2+4\beta_{1}(d-1)+2(d-1)+8\eta d_{h}d^{2}\Big)^{2}
.

Property
\mathcal{B}(T+1)
is established by (Eq. (
35
)), (Eq. (
36
)) and (Eq. (
37
)).

C.5.1
Auxiliary lemma

Lemma C.5

As long as
2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma\leq\ln 2
, and
2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma\leq\ln 2
, we have

\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\leq\frac{3}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))

Proof of lemma
C.5
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T+1}\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\big(\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T-1))-\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T-1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

The first inequality is due to
\exp(\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)
is monotone increasing.
The last inequality is due to
2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma\leq\ln 2
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{-1}^{T}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma}\big(\exp(\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)-\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma}\exp(\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\exp(\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

The first inequality is due to
\exp(-\eta(\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1})\gamma s)
is monotone decreasing.
The fourth inequality is due to
\beta_{2}\geq 4\beta_{1}
, thus
\frac{1}{\beta_{2}-2\beta_{1}}\leq\frac{2}{\beta 2}
.
The last inequality is due to
\eta\beta_{2}\gamma\leq(\ln 2)/2\leq\ln(3/2)
.

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{T+1}\exp(\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma s-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma}\big(\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))-\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma)}{\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))

The first inequality is due to
\exp(\eta(2\beta_{2}-\beta_{1})\gamma s)
is monotone increasing.
The last inequality is due to
\beta_{2}\geq\beta_{1}
and
2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma\leq\ln 2
.

The proof of
\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma(T+1))
is similar to

\sum_{s=0}^{T}\exp(2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(s-T))\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma s)\leq\frac{2}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma(T+1))
.
Just replace
\beta_{1}
with
\beta_{2}
, and consider
2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma\leq\ln 2
.

C.6
Proof of claim
B.3

This Section presents the bounds for terms
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{j}(T+1)
and
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)
with
i,j\in[1,d],i\neq j
,
establishing the property
\mathcal{C}(T+1)
.

Recall the
Vector-coupled Dynamics
equations of
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}_{j}(t+1)
,
\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{c}_{j}(t+1)
and
\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}(t+1)
in lemma
A.7
:

\begin{split}&\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}_{j}(t+1)\\
&=\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)+\eta\Big(2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)+2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\\
&-\beta_{3}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)-2\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\end{split}

(38)

\begin{split}&\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{c}_{j}(t+1)\\
&=\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{c}_{j}(t)+\eta\Big(2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)+2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)\\
&-\beta_{3}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)-2\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\\
&-2\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)\end{split}

(39)

\begin{split}&\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}(t+1)\\
&=\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\\
&-\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\end{split}

(40)

To give bounds for the above three terms, we will use the following bounds from property
\mathcal{B}(t)
and lemma
C.7
:

|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|\leq\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\frac{\delta(t)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|\leq 8\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

Besides, by
\Big|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\Big|\leq\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)
, we have:

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\delta(t)+2\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5\delta(t)}{\alpha^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 6\delta(t)

The third inequality is by
\delta(s)\geq 2\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}\geq 2\alpha=2\alpha=2\exp((-\ln 2)/N)
.
For the last inequality, as long as
N\geq\frac{2\ln 2}{\ln 6-\ln 5}
, we have
\frac{5}{\alpha^{2}}\leq 6
.

We will provide the upper bounds for
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)\Big|
,
\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{j}(T+1)\Big|
and
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)\Big|
by substituting
the above bounds into (Eq.
38
), (Eq.
39
) and (Eq.
40
) respectively.

Bound of
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)\Big|

\begin{split}&\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\eta\Big(2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\\
&-\beta_{3}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)-2\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|+2\eta\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|+2\eta\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big|\\
&+\eta\beta_{3}\Big|\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\Big|+2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\Big|\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|+\eta^{2}\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\delta(T)+4\eta\cdot\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\cdot 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+2\eta\beta_{3}\cdot 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+2\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\cdot\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)^{2}+\eta^{2}\cdot 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\delta_{\max}+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\Big)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\end{split}

(41)

The first inequality is derived by triangle inequality.
The second inequality is derived by
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|\leq\delta(T)
and substituting the bounds of
|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|
,

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|
and
\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|
.
The third inequality is derived by factoring out the common factor
\delta(T)
.
The last inequality is derived by
\delta(T)\leq\delta_{\max}
and
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\leq 1
.

Bound of
\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{j}(T+1)\Big|

\begin{split}&\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{j}(T+1)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{j}(T)+\eta\Big(2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)+2\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\\
&-\beta_{3}\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)-2\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\\
&-2\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{c}_{j}(T)\Big|+2\eta\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big|+2\eta\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|\\
&+\eta\beta_{3}\Big|\big(\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)+\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\Big|+2\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i,k\neq j}^{d}\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{k}(T)\Big|\\
&+2\eta\beta_{2}\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{j}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\Big|+\eta^{2}\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\delta(T)+4\eta\cdot\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\cdot 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+2\eta\beta_{3}\cdot 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+2\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\cdot\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)^{2}\\
&+2\eta\beta_{2}\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)^{2}\\
&+\eta^{2}\cdot\Big(8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(T)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\Big)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)\\
&+\frac{2\eta\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)+8\eta\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
