&+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\beta_{2}\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+40\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(T)\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(16\eta\delta_{\max}+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}+\frac{2\eta\delta_{\max}}{\beta_{2}}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\beta_{2}\delta_{\max}+40\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\end{split}

(42)

The first inequality is derived by triangle inequality.
The second inequality is derived by
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|\leq\delta(T)
and substituting the bounds of
|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|
,

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|
and
\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)\Big|
.
The third inequality is derived by factoring out the common factor
\delta(T)
.
The last inequality is derived by
\delta(T)\leq\delta_{\max}
,
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\leq 1
and
\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\leq 1
.

Bound of
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)\Big|

\begin{split}&\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\\
&-\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big|\\
&=\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\\
&-\beta_{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big)+\eta^{2}\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\Big|+\eta\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\big)\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\Big|+\eta\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\Big|\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\Big|\\
&+\eta\beta_{2}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\Big|\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big|+\eta\beta_{2}\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}(T)\cdot\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{i}(T)\Big|+\eta^{2}\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{\bar{b}}(T)\Big|\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\eta\cdot\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\cdot\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)\\
&+\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\cdot\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)\\
&+\eta\beta_{2}\cdot 6\delta(T)\cdot\Big(2\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)\\
&+\eta^{2}\cdot\Big(8\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)+4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\Big)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(\eta\big(2(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})(d-1)+1\big)\cdot\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+6\eta\beta_{2}\cdot\frac{\delta(T)}{\beta_{2}}+4\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+\delta(T)\Big(2\eta\big(2(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})(d-1)+1\big)\delta(T)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+12\eta\beta_{2}\delta(T)+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(T)\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(4\eta d\delta_{\max}+4\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\beta_{2}d\delta_{\max}+12\eta\beta_{2}\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\end{split}

(43)

The first inequality is derived by triangle inequality.
The second inequality is derived by
\Big|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T)\bm{b}_{j}(T)\Big|\leq\delta(T)
and substituting the bounds of
|\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|
,

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|
,
|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|
and
\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|
.
The third inequality is derived by factoring out the common factor
\delta(T)
.
The last inequality is derived by
\delta(T)\leq\delta_{\max}
,
\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\leq 1
and

2(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})(d-1)+1\leq 4\beta_{2}d
since
\beta_{2}\geq\beta_{1}
and
\beta_{2}\geq 1
.

We next provide the upper bound for
\delta(T+1)
.

\begin{split}&\delta(T+1)=\max\{|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}_{j}(T+1)|,|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{c}_{j}(T+1)|,|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(T+1)\bm{b}(T+1)|\}\\
&\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(16\eta d\delta_{\max}+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}+\frac{2\eta\delta_{\max}}{\beta_{2}}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)\\
&+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\beta_{2}d\delta_{\max}+40\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}+12\eta\beta_{2}\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)\\
\end{split}

(44)

This inequality can be verified by comparing with (Eq. (
41
)), (Eq. (
42
)), (Eq. (
43
)).
To give more precise bound, we introduce the following lemma:

Lemma C.6

If
y(t+1)\leq y(t)+cy(t)\exp(-at)+dy(t)\exp(-bt)
, with
a,b,c,d>0,t\geq 0
and
a,b\leq\ln 2
, then
y(t)
satisfies:

y(t)\leq y(0)\exp(\frac{2c}{a}+\frac{2d}{b})

Proof of lemma
C.6
.

\displaystyle y(t+1)\leq y(t)+cy(t)\exp(-at)+dy(t)\exp(-bt)

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow y(t+1)\leq y(t)(1+c\exp(-at)+d\exp(-bt))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow y(t+1)\leq y(0)\prod_{s=0}^{t}(1+c\exp(-as)+d\exp(-bs))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\ln y(t+1)\leq\ln y(0)+\sum_{s=0}^{t}\ln(1+c\exp(-as)+d\exp(-bs))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\ln y(t+1)\leq\ln y(0)+\sum_{s=0}^{t}(c\exp(-as)+d\exp(-bs))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\ln y(t+1)\leq\ln y(0)+\int_{-1}^{t}(c\exp(-as)+d\exp(-bs))ds

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\ln y(t+1)\leq\ln y(0)+(\frac{c}{a}(\exp(a)-\exp(-at))+\frac{d}{b}(\exp(b)-\exp(-bt)))

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\ln y(t+1)\leq\ln y(0)+(\frac{2c}{a}+\frac{2d}{b})

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow y(t+1)\leq y(0)\exp(\frac{2c}{a}+\frac{2d}{b})

The fourth arrow is due to
\ln(1+x)\leq x
for
x\geq 0
.
The fifth arrow is due to
\exp(-as),\exp(-bs)
are monotone decreasing.
the 7-th arrow is due to
a,b\leq\ln 2
and
-\exp(-at)\leq 0
,
-\exp(-bt)\leq 0
.

Lemma
C.6
presents the core idea of establishing property
\mathcal{C}(T+1)
.
If
a\gg c
and
b\gg d
in the above lemma,
we will have
y(t+1)\leq y(0)\cdot O(1)
.
Similarly, as Mamba converges quickly (
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}\rightarrow\frac{\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}\bm{I}
,
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{b}\rightarrow\bm{0}
),
we can prove that
|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|,|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{c}_{j}(t)|,|\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)|
hold their magnitudes around their initial states.

We next combine (Eq. (
44
)) and lemma
C.6
to give bound for
\delta(T+1)
.

\displaystyle\delta(T+1)

\displaystyle\leq\delta(T)+\delta(T)\Big(16\eta d\delta_{\max}+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}+\frac{2\eta\delta_{\max}}{\beta_{2}}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma T)

\displaystyle+\delta(T)\Big(8\eta\beta_{2}d\delta_{\max}+40\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}+12\eta\beta_{2}\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\Big)\cdot\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma T)

\displaystyle\leq\delta(0)\cdot\exp\Big(\frac{2\big(16\eta d\delta_{\max}+4\eta\beta_{3}+8\eta\beta_{1}(d-2)\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}+\frac{2\eta\delta_{\max}}{\beta_{2}}\big)}{\eta\beta_{1}\gamma}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\big(8\eta\beta_{2}d\delta_{\max}+40\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta_{\max}+12\eta\beta_{2}\delta_{\max}+8\eta^{2}\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta_{\max}\big)}{\eta\beta_{2}\gamma}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\delta(0)\cdot\exp\Big(\frac{3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}}{\frac{1}{2}d_{h}}\cdot\Big(\frac{32d}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{8\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}+16(d-2)+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{4}{\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle+16d+80\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}+24+16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\Big)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{2}\cdot\delta(0)

\displaystyle\leq 3\sqrt{d_{h}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)}

The first inequality is derived by (Eq. (
44
)).
The second inequality is derived by lemma
C.6
.
The third inequality is derived by
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}d_{h}
.
The last inequality is derived by

\displaystyle d_{h}=\widetilde{\Omega}(d^{2})

\displaystyle\geq\frac{36}{(\ln(3/2))^{2}}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(\frac{32d}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{8\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle+16(d-2)+\frac{8}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{4}{\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}+16d+80\ln 2+24+8\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{36}{(\ln(3/2))^{2}}\log(4d(2d+1)/\delta)\Big(\frac{32d}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{8\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle+16(d-2)+\frac{16\eta d_{h}d^{2}}{\beta_{1}}+\frac{4}{\beta_{1}\beta_{2}}+16d+80\eta\beta_{2}d_{h}+24+16\eta d_{h}d^{2}\Big)^{2}

C.7
Bounds of
\eta^{2}
terms

This Section presents the bounds for

\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)
,
\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)
,
\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|_{2}^{2}
,

\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)
,
\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)
,
\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)

(these terms usually appear in the
Vector-coupled Dynamics
equations with a
\eta^{2}
factor) with
i,j\in[1,d]

under the assumption that
\mathcal{A}(t)
,
\mathcal{B}(t)
, and
\mathcal{C}(t)
hold.

Lemma C.7

Under the assumption that
\mathcal{A}(t)
,
\mathcal{B}(t)
, and
\mathcal{C}(t)
hold, we have the following bounds:

\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+24\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),

\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|_{2}^{2}\leq 16d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|\leq 4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+28\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),

\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|\leq 8\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

where
i,j\in[1,d]
. Note that this lemma does not require
i\neq j
.

Firstly, recall the following dynamics equation in lemma
A.6
:

\displaystyle\bm{b}_{i}(t+1)

\displaystyle=\bm{b}_{i}(t)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big)

\displaystyle=:\bm{b}_{i}(t)+\eta\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)

\displaystyle\bm{c}_{i}(t+1)

\displaystyle=\bm{c}_{i}(t)+\eta\Big(\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{b}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}(t)

\displaystyle-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{b}(t)\Big)

\displaystyle=:\bm{c}_{i}(t)+\eta\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)

\displaystyle\bm{b}(t+1)

\displaystyle=\bm{b}(t)-\eta\Big(\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big)=:\bm{b}(t)+\eta\bm{\bar{b}}(t)

Thus we have

\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)=\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)

\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)=\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{b}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}(t)-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{b}(t)

\bm{\bar{b}}(t)=\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)

Recalling the properties
\mathcal{A}(t)
and
\mathcal{B}(t)
:

\mathcal{A}(t):

d_{h}/2\leq\bm{b}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t),\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{c}_{i}(t),\bm{b}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\leq 2d_{h}

\mathcal{B}(t):

|\beta_{3}/\beta_{1}-\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)|\leq\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{j}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)|\leq 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\frac{\delta(t)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

We can derive the follow bounds for the norm of
\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)
,
\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)
and
\bm{\bar{b}}(t)
:

\displaystyle\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)\Big\|=\Big\|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{c}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\bm{c}_{i}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\Big|+\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\Big\|\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d_{h}}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\beta_{1}\leq 1
.

\displaystyle\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)\Big\|=\Big\|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\bm{b}_{i}(t)-\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\cdot\bm{b}_{k}(t)-\beta_{2}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{b}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\bm{b}_{i}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\big(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{i}(t)\big)\Big|+\beta_{1}\sum_{k\neq i}^{d}\Big\|\bm{b}_{k}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}_{k}(t)\Big|+\beta_{2}\Big\|\bm{b}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\bm{c}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d_{h}}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{1}(d-1)\cdot 2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}\cdot\Big(2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\frac{\delta(t)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\beta_{1}\leq 1
.

\displaystyle\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|=\Big\|\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\cdot\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\beta_{2}\sum_{k=1}^{d}\Big\|\bm{c}_{k}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big|\bm{c}_{k}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)\Big|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}d\cdot\Big(2\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\frac{\delta(t)}{\beta_{2}}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

By multiplying them pairwise, we obtain:

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)^{2}\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)
.

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)^{\top}\bm{\bar{c}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

\displaystyle+16d_{h}\beta_{2}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+40\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\beta_{2}d\leq 2\beta_{2}^{2}
because
\beta_{2}=\Omega(d^{2})\geq d
,
and
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)
.

\displaystyle\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|_{2}^{2}\leq\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)^{2}

\displaystyle=8d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\displaystyle+8d_{h}\beta_{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 16d_{h}\beta_{2}^{2}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+2d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\beta_{2}\leq\beta_{2}^{2}
because
\beta_{2}\geq 1
,
and
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)
.

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big|\leq\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{j}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\Big)\cdot 2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 8d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)
.

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|\leq\Big\|\bm{\bar{c}}_{i}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\cdot\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle=4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+8\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

\displaystyle+8\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)+4\beta_{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)

\displaystyle\leq 4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)+28\beta_{2}^{2}d_{h}d\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\beta_{2}d\leq 2\beta_{2}^{2}
,
\beta_{2}\leq\beta_{2}^{2}
,
and
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-2\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)
.

\displaystyle\Big|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}^{\top}(t)\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big|\leq\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}_{i}(t)\Big\|\cdot\Big\|\bm{\bar{b}}(t)\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\cdot\Big(2\sqrt{2d_{h}}\beta_{2}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+\sqrt{2d_{h}}d\delta(t)\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\Big)

\displaystyle\leq 8\beta_{2}d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)+4d_{h}d^{2}\delta(t)^{2}\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)

The last inequality is by
\exp(-2\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{1}\gamma t),\quad\exp(-\eta(\beta_{1}+\beta_{2})\gamma t)\leq\exp(-\eta\beta_{2}\gamma t)
.

Appendix D
Discussion

In this section, we show that orthogonal initialization Mamba can be trained to ICL solution, and compare our method with some previous works.

Orthogonal Initialization

Now we assume that each column of
\bm{W}_{B}
and
\bm{W}_{C}
are initialized with orthogonal columns of unit norm.
Then we have

\bm{C}^{\top}(0)\bm{C}(0)=\bm{B}^{\top}(0)\bm{B}(0)=\bm{I},\quad\bm{B}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)=\bm{C}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)=\bm{0}.

Consider the following update rule as part of lemma
5.1
.

\bm{B}(t+1)=\bm{B}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{C}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t),

(45)

\bm{C}(t+1)=\bm{C}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{B}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{B}(t)\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{b}(t)\bm{b}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t),

(46)

\bm{b}(t+1)=\bm{b}(t)-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t).

(47)

By (Eq. (
45
)), (Eq. (
46
)) and (Eq. (
47
)), we have:

\displaystyle\bm{B}^{\top}(t+1)\bm{b}(t+1)=\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)

\displaystyle-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)-\eta^{2}\beta_{2}\beta_{3}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)+\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)

\displaystyle\bm{C}(t+1)^{\top}\bm{b}(t+1)=\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t)\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)\bm{b}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)

\displaystyle-\eta\beta_{2}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)-\eta^{2}\beta_{2}\beta_{3}\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)

\displaystyle+\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{2}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t)\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)+\eta^{2}\beta_{2}^{2}\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)\bm{b}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{b}(t)

Combining
\bm{B}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)=\bm{C}^{\top}(0)\bm{b}(0)=\bm{0}
with induction, we can derive that
\bm{B}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)=\bm{C}^{\top}(t)\bm{b}(t)=\bm{0}
for
t\geq 0
.
Thus we only need to consider the following dynamics.

\bm{B}(t+1)=\bm{B}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{C}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{C}(t)\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t),

(48)

\bm{C}(t+1)=\bm{C}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}\bm{B}(t)-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{B}(t)\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)

(49)

Denote
\bm{B}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t)=D(t)
,
\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{C}(t)=E(t)
and
\bm{C}(t)^{\top}\bm{B}(t)=F(t)

then by (Eq. (
48
)) and (Eq. (
49
)), we have

\begin{split}\bm{F}(t+1)&=\bm{F}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}(\bm{D}(t)+\bm{E}(t))-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{F}(t)\bm{D}(t)\\
&+\eta^{2}\beta_{3}^{2}\bm{F}(t)^{\top}-2\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{3}\bm{F}(t)\bm{F}(t)^{\top}-\eta\beta_{1}\bm{E}(t)\bm{F}(t)\\
&+\eta^{2}\beta_{1}^{2}\bm{F}(t)\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{F}(t)\end{split}

(50)

\begin{split}\bm{D}(t+1)&=\bm{D}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}(\bm{F}(t)+\bm{F}(t)^{\top})-\eta\beta_{1}(\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{F}(t)+\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{F}(t))\\
&+\eta^{2}\beta_{3}^{2}\bm{E}(t)-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{3}\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{E}(t)\\
&-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{3}\bm{E}(t)\bm{F}(t)+\eta^{2}\beta_{1}^{2}\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{E}(t)\bm{F}(t)\end{split}

(51)

\begin{split}\bm{E}(t+1)&=\bm{E}(t)+\eta\beta_{3}(\bm{F}(t)+\bm{F}(t)^{\top})-\eta\beta_{1}(\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{F}(t)+\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{F}(t))\\
&+\eta^{2}\beta_{3}^{2}\bm{D}(t)-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{3}\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{D}(t)\\
&-\eta^{2}\beta_{1}\beta_{3}\bm{D}(t)\bm{F}(t)+\eta^{2}\beta_{1}^{2}\bm{F}(t)^{\top}\bm{D}(t)\bm{F}(t)\end{split}

(52)

Note that
D(0)=E(0)=\bm{I}
and
F(0)=\bm{0}
.
By induction we can see that
D(t),E(t)
and
F(t)
are diagonal matrix for
t>0
.
Because of the symmetry, we have
D(t)=E(t)
.
Now we denote
D(t)=E(t)=g(t)\bm{I}
and
F(t)=h(t)\bm{I}
.
Then based on (Eq. (
50
)), (Eq. (
51
)) and (Eq. (
52
)), we have:

g(t+1)=g(t)+\eta(2h(t)+\eta\beta_{3}g(t)-\eta\beta_{1}g(t)h(t))(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}h(t))

(53)

h(t+1)=h(t)+\eta g(t)(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}h(t))+\eta^{2}h(t)(\beta_{3}-\beta_{1}h(t))^{2}

(54)

Since
g(0)=1
and
h(0)=0
at initialization,
h(t)
will converge to
\frac{\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}
(i.e.
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}\rightarrow\frac{\beta_{3}}{\beta_{1}}\bm{I}
).

Compare with Other Techniques

(Eq. (
45
)), (Eq. (
46
)) and (Eq. (
47
)) can be viewed as the gradient descent that minimize the following target:

\frac{1}{2}\|\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{W}_{B}\bm{X}-\bm{Y}\|_{F}^{2}

(55)

where
\bm{X}
and
\bm{Y}
satisfy:

\bm{X}\bm{X}^{\top}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta_{1}&&&\\
&\ddots&&\\
&&\beta_{1}&\\
&&&\beta_{2}\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{(d+1)\times(d+1)},\bm{X}\bm{Y}^{\top}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta_{3}&&&\\
&\ddots&&\\
&&\beta_{3}&\\
&&\bm{0}_{1\times d}&\end{bmatrix}\in\mathbb{R}^{(d+1)\times(d)}.

To establish convergence for this problem under gaussian initialization,
Arora et al., 2019
require the standard deviation to be small enough,
while
Du and Hu, 2019
require larger dimension
d_{h}
because their method relies on the condition number of
\bm{X}
.
Our method balances the requirements on initialization and dimension.
The fine-grained nature of our analysis (particularly the
Vector-coupled Dynamics
) enables extension to various problem beyong (Eq. (
55
)).

Appendix E
Additional Experimental Results

(a)

Initialization

(b)

Trained parameter

(c)

Loss curve

Figure 2
:

(a) Visualization of matrix product
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{W}_{B}
before training;
(b) Post-training visualization of matrix product
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{W}_{B}
;
(c) Test loss versus token sequence length
N
.
Blue curve: experimental loss; orange dashed line: theoretical loss
\frac{d}{2}\Big(1-\frac{\beta_{3}^{2}}{\beta_{1}}\Big)
.

Experiments Setting

We follow Section
3
to generate the dateset and initialize the model.
Specifically, we set dimension
d=4
,
d_{h}=80
, prompt token length
N=50
, and train the Mamba model on
3000
sequences by gradient descent.
Moreover, we vary the length of the prompt token
N
from
4
to
80
and compare the test loss with the theoretical loss.
For each
N
, we conduct 10 independent experiments and report the averaged results.
All experiments are performed on an NVIDIA A800 GPU.

Experiment Result

Figure
2(a)
and Figure
2(b)
show that
\bm{C}^{\top}\bm{B}
can be trained to diagonal matrix from random initialization.
Figure
2(c)
show that the experimental loss aligns with the theoretical loss
\mathcal{L}(\bm{\theta})=\frac{d}{2}\Big(1-\frac{\beta_{3}^{2}}{\beta_{1}}\Big)
,
noting that the theoretical loss
\Big(1-\frac{\beta_{3}^{2}}{\beta_{1}}\Big)
has an upper bound
\frac{3d(d+1)}{2N}
that decays linearly with N.
These experimental results further verified our theoretical proof.

Mamba vs Linear Attention

Optimal linear attention outperforms Mamba under our construction,
and they have
O(1/N)
error upper bound with different constant factors.
We provide a comprarison of loss between optimal Mamba (under our Assumption 4.1) with optimal linear attention as in Table
2
with setting
d=10,N=10,20,\dots,80
.

When N is smaller than d

We also test the case when
N\leq d
in Table
3
with setting
d=20,N=4,6,\dots,20
.

Convergence of
\bm{w}_{\Delta}

We set
\bm{w}_{\Delta}=0
in the assumption. Now we show that random initializd
\bm{w}_{\Delta}=0
can converge to 0 experimental. The results is in Table
4
.

Different
d_{h}

Table
5
shows the mean value and standard deviation of the loss for smaller
d_{h}
(in 10 repeated experiments). We set
d=4,N=30
, and the theoretical loss is 0.2954.
